# ADVANCED TOPICS ROADMAP

---

## Beyond the 10-Day Program

---

Students who complete this certification are prepared for advanced study in the following areas.

---

## Module 11-12: Generative AI and Large Language Models (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 11.1 Introduction to LLMs | How ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini work; Transformer architecture; Attention mechanisms |
| 11.2 Prompt Engineering | Crafting prompts; Few-shot learning; Chain-of-thought; Safety |
| 11.3 Working with APIs | OpenAI API; Building chatbots; Document Q&A; Content generation |
| 11.4 Fine-tuning LLMs | When to fine-tune; Training data; Process; Evaluation |

**Projects:** Custom chatbot, Document summarizer, Code assistant, Content generator

---

## Module 13-14: Computer Vision Deep Dive (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 13.1 Advanced CNNs | ResNet, VGG, EfficientNet; Skip connections; Batch normalization |
| 13.2 Object Detection | YOLO; R-CNN family; Bounding boxes; Non-maximum suppression |
| 13.3 Image Segmentation | Semantic segmentation; Instance segmentation; U-Net; Medical imaging |
| 13.4 Vision Projects | Face recognition; Object tracking; Pose estimation; Real-time apps |

**Projects:** Safety equipment detection, Medical image analysis, Autonomous perception, AR features

---

## Module 15-16: Natural Language Processing (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 15.1 Text Processing | Tokenization; Word embeddings (Word2Vec, GloVe); TF-IDF |
| 15.2 NLP Tasks | Sentiment analysis; Named Entity Recognition; Classification |
| 15.3 Advanced NLP | RNNs; LSTMs and GRUs; Sequence-to-sequence; Attention |
| 15.4 Modern NLP | BERT, GPT; Hugging Face; Fine-tuning; Zero-shot classification |

**Projects:** Sentiment analyzer, Intent chatbot, Text summarizer, Language translator

---

## Module 17-18: AI Model Deployment and MLOps (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 17.1 Model Serving | Flask API; FastAPI; Docker containerization; REST best practices |
| 17.2 Cloud Deployment | AWS SageMaker; Google Cloud AI; Azure ML; Serverless |
| 17.3 MLOps Fundamentals | Model versioning (MLflow, DVC); Experiment tracking; CI/CD |
| 17.4 Monitoring | Drift detection; Performance monitoring; Retraining strategies |

**Projects:** Web service, Prediction API, Dockerized app, CI/CD pipeline

---

## Module 19-20: Multimodal AI and Advanced Applications (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 19.1 Text-to-Image | DALL-E, Midjourney, Stable Diffusion; Diffusion models; Prompting |
| 19.2 Text-to-Video/Audio | Video generation; Text-to-speech; Voice cloning; Music |
| 19.3 Multimodal Models | CLIP; Vision-language models; Cross-modal retrieval; Captioning |
| 19.4 Emerging Apps | 3D generation; Virtual avatars; Real-time translation; Accessibility |

**Projects:** Custom image generator, Voice assistant, Multi-modal search, Creative AI app

---

## Module 21-22: Robotics AI Integration (6 hours) — ACCN Special

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 21.1 CV for Robots | Object recognition; Depth perception; Visual servoing; SLAM |
| 21.2 Sensor Fusion | Camera + LIDAR + IMU; Kalman filters; Multi-sensor decisions |
| 21.3 Path Planning | A* algorithm; Neural network planning; Obstacle avoidance; RL navigation |
| 21.4 ROS Integration | Robot Operating System; ML with ROS; Gazebo simulation |

**Projects:** Robot arm recognition, Autonomous navigation, Multi-sensor perception, Robot AI integration

---

## Module 23-24: Reinforcement Learning (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 23.1 RL Fundamentals | Markov Decision Processes; Rewards; Exploration vs exploitation; Q-Learning |
| 23.2 Deep RL | DQN; Policy gradients; Actor-Critic; PPO |
| 23.3 RL Frameworks | OpenAI Gym; Stable Baselines3; Ray RLlib; Environments |
| 23.4 Advanced Apps | Game AI; Robotics control; Resource optimization; AlphaGo |

**Projects:** Atari game agent, Cartpole balance, Custom environment, Robot RL control

---

## Module 25-26: AI Ethics and Safety Advanced (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 25.1 AI Alignment | Value alignment; Reward hacking; Specification gaming |
| 25.2 Explainable AI | LIME; SHAP; Attention visualization; Interpretability |
| 25.3 Adversarial ML | Adversarial examples; Attack methods; Defense techniques; Robustness |
| 25.4 AI Governance | Regulatory frameworks; Auditing; Fairness metrics; Responsible deployment |

**Projects:** Explain model decisions, Test robustness, Fairness audit, Ethics case study

---

## Module 27-28: AI Research and Career Development (6 hours)

---

| Session | Topics |
|---------|--------|
| 27.1 Reading Papers | How to read efficiently; Understanding methodology; arXiv |
| 27.2 Implementing Papers | Choosing papers; Coding from math; Debugging; Sharing |
| 27.3 Kaggle Competitions | Strategies; Feature engineering; Ensembles; Leaderboards |
| 27.4 Portfolio Building | GitHub best practices; Technical blogging; Portfolio website |
| 28.1 Career Paths | ML Engineer, Data Scientist, AI Researcher, MLOps, PM, Prompt Engineer |
| 28.2 Interview Prep | Technical interviews; Coding challenges; System design |
| 28.3 Networking | Conferences; Online communities; Social media presence |
| 28.4 Continuous Learning | Advanced courses; Research groups; Newsletters; Habits |

## Complete Learning Path

---

```
10-Day Foundation (30 hours)
         ↓
Choose Specialization (30-60 hours)
    ├── Generative AI & LLMs (Modules 11-12)
    ├── Computer Vision (Modules 13-14)
    ├── NLP (Modules 15-16)
    ├── Robotics AI (Modules 21-22)
    └── Reinforcement Learning (Modules 23-24)
         ↓
Core Advanced Topics (30 hours)
    ├── Deployment & MLOps (Modules 17-18)
    ├── Multimodal AI (Modules 19-20)
    └── Ethics & Safety (Modules 25-26)
         ↓
Career Development (12 hours)
    └── Research & Career (Modules 27-28)
         ↓
Continuous Practice
    ├── Kaggle Competitions
    ├── Personal Projects
    ├── Open Source Contributions
    └── Research Paper Implementations
```

**Total Professional Path:** 100+ hours structured learning + continuous practice

---

## Recommended Learning Sequences

---

| Path | Name | Sequence |
|------|------|----------|
| 1 | Generative AI Specialist | Foundation → LLMs (11-12) → Multimodal (19-20) → MLOps (17-18) → Ethics (25-26) → Career (27-28) |
| 2 | Computer Vision Engineer | Foundation → CV (13-14) → Multimodal (19-20) → MLOps (17-18) → Ethics (25-26) → Career (27-28) |
| 3 | NLP Engineer | Foundation → NLP (15-16) → LLMs (11-12) → MLOps (17-18) → Ethics (25-26) → Career (27-28) |
| 4 | Robotics AI Engineer | Foundation → Robotics (21-22) → CV (13-14) → RL (23-24) → MLOps (17-18) → Career (27-28) |
| 5 | ML Engineering Generalist | Foundation → MLOps (17-18) → 2 Specializations → Ethics (25-26) → Career (27-28) |

# PROGRAM COMPLETION

---

**Congratulations on exploring this comprehensive AI curriculum!**

This 10-day foundation plus advanced modules pathway represents a complete journey from beginner to competent AI practitioner. Students who complete this program will be equipped to:

- Build real-world AI applications
- Continue learning independently
- Contribute to AI projects professionally
- Make informed decisions about AI technology
- Consider ethical implications of AI systems
- Pursue advanced study or careers in AI

**For ACCN students:** This curriculum integrates with your learning journey, providing AI capabilities for computer vision, autonomous navigation, and intelligent decision-making.

---

**The future of AI is in your hands. Build responsibly, ethically, and creatively!**
